#7581. Mercury: Creative Director - Brand Narrative

Headquarters: San Francisco, CA, New York, NY, Portland, OR, or Remote within Canada or United States

In 1977, Charles and Ray Eames began a film with a picnic beside Chicago’s lakefront. Over the next nine minutes, Powers of Ten traveled out toward distant galaxies and back into the microscopic world inside a human hand. An enormous subject became a journey you could follow, built around a simple change in perspective. Images, language, movement, and sound brought the idea to life together. 

That’s the kind of creative thinking we’re looking for at Mercury: finding a compelling idea inside something complex, then bringing together the people and crafts that make it unforgettable.

We’re looking for a Creative Director to build and lead Brand Narrative, a new team within Brand Creative. You’ll turn company strategy, product truths, customer insight, and cultural observations into original concepts for our most important launches, campaigns, and brand moments. You’ll originate ideas yourself and stay closely involved as they become finished work.

Your background might be in art direction, design, or copywriting. What matters is your ability to develop exceptional concepts, recognize the best idea wherever it comes from, and bring the whole creative studio together to make it real. Whatever your starting discipline, you’ll bring deep experience in film-led storytelling.

You’ll lead Brand Narrative in close creative partnership with your peers leading Brand Design and Writing. Together, you’ll shape concepts, language, and visual expression from the beginning, bringing distinct perspectives and shared ownership to the work. You’ll contribute strong ideas of your own, invite others to challenge and improve them, and build on theirs. This is a role for someone who enjoys making work together, not developing ideas in isolation and handing them off.

You’ll also shape what Brand Narrative becomes. That means building its culture, developing its people, and establishing ways of working that make the entire studio more creatively ambitious. The opportunity is to make great work while building a team that can keep making it.

What you’ll do

In your first year, your focus will be roughly 70% leading creative development for our highest-priority launches and campaigns, and 30% building the Brand Narrative function.

  • Originate and lead big creative ideas. Partner with Product Marketing and Brand Marketing on the brief, then turn the agreed audience, opportunity, and product proof into original creative concepts. Develop ideas that can take distinctive forms across film, social, out-of-home, digital, experiences, and partnerships.
  • Bring ambitious concepts to life with your creative peers and the full studio. Work side by side with the Brand Design and Writing leads and their teams from the earliest explorations through delivery. Develop ideas together, exchange thoughtful critique, and make creative choices that draw on each discipline’s expertise. Stay hands-on, contribute through your own craft, and partner closely with Motion and Production throughout. For film, remain closely involved through scripts, visual development, production, and the final edit.
  • Build audience and distribution thinking into the idea. Shape concepts around how people will discover, watch, and share the work. Work with marketing partners to consider channels and viewing contexts from the beginning, so the work belongs where people encounter it rather than simply being resized to fit.
  • Push beyond the first good answer, and learn quickly. Lead thoughtful concept development and critique. Use rough scripts, prototypes, and AI tools to test ideas quickly, learn, and move the strongest work forward. Know when to keep exploring and when to commit, then protect what makes an idea interesting through feedback and production.
  • Find opportunities before a brief arrives. Bring curiosity about Mercury’s products, customers, and the wider world. Develop proactive ideas and cultural opportunities that give the brand something meaningful to say or do.
  • Help decide where creative ambition matters most. Partner early with Product Marketing, Brand Marketing, Product Design, Comms, and Insights. Help identify which moments deserve our largest creative investment, present recommendations to senior leadership, and use results from marketing and insights partners to inform what comes next.
  • Build and lead Brand Narrative. Define the team’s purpose, priorities, creative standards, and ways of working. Help hire and develop the team, shaping its structure and capabilities. Set clear expectations, and coach people to grow their judgment and conceptual strength.
  • Create a culture where the best idea wins. Make room for experimentation, honest feedback, and contributions across disciplines. Lead with generosity and conviction, share ownership of the work, and help people challenge one another without making creativity a competition for credit.
  • Build practices that strengthen the work. Establish a clear path from insight and an agreed brief to concept and execution, with useful critique and close collaboration throughout. Develop relationships with external creatives and agencies who can add a distinctive perspective or capability when needed.

What you’ll bring

  • A strong creative foundation in art direction, design, or copywriting. Your portfolio shows original concepts developed into ambitious, well-crafted work across multiple formats. You can explain what you contributed, how the idea evolved, and how collaboration made it better.
  • Deep film experience. You’ve developed film-led ideas from concept through production, with strong instincts for story, scripts, performance, pacing, and sound. You understand how these choices work together to make an audience feel something, not just make a film look good.
  • A track record of deep creative collaboration with design and writing peers. You’ve led ambitious multidisciplinary work from concept through production by building trusted partnerships. You bring a clear point of view, listen closely, work through creative disagreements constructively, and recognize when someone else’s approach makes the work stronger. You can show how your collaborators shaped the outcome, not just what you personally originated.
  • Strong taste, cultural awareness, and product curiosity. You can get close to a complex business or product, find the human truth within it, and turn that understanding into something clear, surprising, and memorable.
  • Experience developing creative people and shaping how a team works. You give specific, useful feedback, set a high bar with care, and create an environment where people can take creative risks. You’re excited to build a new function, including the everyday practices that give it a culture.
  • Clear communication and sound judgment. You can rally people around an ambitious concept, explain why it deserves investment, and navigate competing perspectives without losing the idea. You take ownership in ambiguous situations and balance creative ambition with the need to move work forward.

What success looks like

A year in, you’ll have led major launches and film-led campaigns from original concept through finished work, with Brand Design and Writing as creative partners from the beginning. The studio’s disciplines will have shaped the work together, with audience and distribution informing the creative approach.

Brand Narrative will have a clear identity, shared creative standards, and working practices that help people test and develop stronger ideas together. Alongside planned launches, you’ll have established a pipeline of proactive opportunities that gives Mercury more ways to express its product ambition with originality and cultural relevance.

Please include a portfolio featuring film-led work and broader campaign concepts. We’d love to understand your role in developing the ideas, the choices you made through production, and how you brought the work to life with others.

Please note: This role requires On-Call support during weekends and holidays, and is expected to include frequent travel. The scope may also require reviewing graphic, controversial, and offensive content in accordance with Roblox’s handling policies.

  • Listen to and understand the concerns of Kansas parents, caregivers, and educators regarding the safety of Roblox. You will provide guidance and education that empower these audiences and build confidence in Roblox’s emphasis on product and policy safety, which keeps users safe and healthy.

#7583. Netlify: Senior Marketing Operations Manager

Headquarters: Remote

About the Team

Netlify is a product-led growth company, and Revenue Operations owns the systems, data, and infrastructure that turn that growth into revenue.  With a large volume of people discovering and signing up for Netlify every day, Marketing Operations is critical to understanding those users, connecting activity across our systems, and creating scalable ways to engage them throughout their journey.

As Senior Marketing Operations Manager, you’ll own the operational foundation behind that work. You’ll connect HubSpot, Salesforce, product data, and analytics, build the systems and lifecycle infrastructure that turn signals into action, and give Marketing and our GTM partners trusted visibility into performance. Reporting to the Senior Director of Revenue Operations, you’ll translate the Marketing Operations vision into a roadmap and independently drive it forward while partnering closely with Growth, RevOps, Data, Product, and other GTM teams.

What You’ll Do

  • Execute on the Marketing Operations roadmap, translating broader Marketing and GTM priorities into scalable systems, processes, and programs.
  • Own our marketing automation platform end to end, including workflows, lifecycle stages, segmentation, forms, lists, email operations, data hygiene, deliverability, governance, and documentation.
  • Build and maintain the connection between marketing automation, Salesforce, product data, and analytics so we can understand and engage users throughout the customer journey.
  • Establish reliable attribution and measurement across marketing and product-led journeys, including UTM governance and connections between campaigns, signups, activation, conversion, and revenue.
  • Build reporting and self-service dashboards that give Marketing and GTM partners a trusted view of performance and support weekly, monthly, and executive-level decision-making.
  • Partner with Growth to operationalize lifecycle programs across email, in-product experiences, and sales-assist triggers, turning customer and product signals into scalable journeys from signup through activation and conversion.
  • Build experimentation infrastructure that allows marketers to launch, measure, and learn from tests without requiring engineering support for every experiment.
  • Own the operational health of the MarTech stack, including integrations, data flows, governance, vendor relationships, and periodic assessments of what we should keep, retire, consolidate, or integrate.
  • Establish scalable practices for high-volume email operations, including audience management, domain and inbox health, deliverability, and safeguards that protect the customer experience.
  • Turn large and sometimes ambiguous datasets into actionable recommendations, identifying points of friction or drop-off and partnering with Marketing, Growth, Product, Data, and RevOps on opportunities to improve conversion.
  • Own the Marketing Operations portion of the broader Marketing budget and evaluate technology investments based on business value, scalability, and ROI.
  • Explore emerging capabilities in AI-enabled marketing operations and AI visibility measurement, helping the team establish useful workflows and baselines as the space develops.

What You’ll Bring

  • 7+ years of experience in Marketing Operations or a closely related discipline, with experience operating independently in a complex B2B SaaS or product-led growth environment.
  • Deep experience with marketing automation platforms and CRM systems such as HubSpot, Marketo, Eloqua, and Salesforce, including lifecycle stages, campaign architecture, attribution, lead or account routing, and data governance.
  • Experience working with high-volume customer or prospect datasets and designing segmentation, lifecycle, and operational approaches that remain reliable at scale.
  • Significant experience operating high-volume email programs, with a practical understanding of deliverability, domain and inbox reputation, audience management, warming strategies, and the risks that emerge at large sending volumes.
  • Strong analytical skills and the ability to investigate complex datasets, identify meaningful patterns, question the quality of the underlying data, and turn findings into recommendations.
  • Experience building or operating lifecycle, nurture, or in-product engagement programs tied to measurable activation or conversion outcomes.
  • Systems thinking and experience designing integrations across marketing automation, CRM, product, analytics, and data systems.
  • Experience building measurement and experimentation infrastructure that enables other teams to operate and learn independently.
  • Strong GTM fluency and an understanding of how Marketing Operations, Growth, Sales, RevOps, Product, and Customer Success connect to customer experience and revenue.
  • Comfort using AI-enabled tools and workflows to accelerate your work while applying judgment to validate outputs and determine where automation is (and isn’t) appropriate.
  • Familiarity with product analytics tools such as Amplitude and experience with emerging AEO or AI-visibility tooling is helpful.
  • Experience working with contractors, agencies, or distributed partners is helpful but not required.
  • Familiarity with developer tools, web platforms, or another high-volume PLG business is useful, but we value relevant operating experience over category-specific expertise.

This Role Is a Great Fit If

  • You enjoy building the foundation, not just operating what someone else has already built. You’re energized by taking an imperfect system and creating something scalable, measurable, and trusted.
  • You’re comfortable moving between architecture and execution. You can define how a system should work and then get into the details yourself when necessary to make it work.
  • You thrive with significant autonomy. Given a goal and context, you investigate the problem, weigh tradeoffs, make a recommendation, and drive the work forward without needing a detailed playbook.
  • You genuinely enjoy data. Large, messy datasets make you curious, and you’re willing to dig until you understand what is happening and what the business should do about it.
  • You care deeply about operational quality. Data integrity, attribution, documentation, audience segmentation, and email deliverability aren’t afterthoughts—they’re part of building a trustworthy growth engine.
  • You balance speed with judgment. You know when to move quickly, when a shortcut creates unacceptable downstream risk, and when to challenge an approach that won’t scale.
  • You see AI as a way to amplify good judgment and increase leverage rather than a substitute for understanding the work.
  • You communicate clearly in an async, distributed environment and can build alignment across Marketing, Growth, Product, Sales, RevOps, and Data without relying on organizational authority.

Headquarters: Remote

Storyblok is a headless CMS that enables marketers and developers to create with joy and publish with confidence. It empowers you to deliver structured and consistent content everywhere: websites, apps, AI search, and beyond.

Legendary brands like Virgin Media O2, Oatly, and TomTom use Storyblok to make a bigger, faster market impact.

 

WHAT IS IN IT FOR YOU

You will be joining a growing company where you can contribute to many “firsts”. Plus these benefits:

  • Monthly remote work stipend (home internet costs, electricity). Home office equipment package right at the start (laptop, keyboard, monitor…)

JOB SUMMARY

Storyblok is hiring a Marketing Automation Manager to own the top of funnel systems and the campaigns that run through them: Pardot, the forms on the Storyblok website, list uploads, Clay, and the nurture programs, sequences and webinar flows built in Pardot. The role sends the campaigns, loads the lists and builds the forms day to day, and turns that repeat work into templates and automations so more of the week goes to lead scoring, routing, data quality and reporting. Sitting within Revenue Operations, this role works across Demand Generation, Events, Content, Partner Marketing, Sales Development, Sales Operations and Analytics, in a lean data team where the person pulls their own numbers and builds their own reports.

 ESSENTIAL JOB FUNCTIONS

  1. Campaign operations
  • Build and send list emails and sequences in Pardot and Knak: webinar invites and follow ups, quarterly enterprise and marketer sequences, review asks, partner and event emails.
  • Build and run the nurture programs in Engagement Studio (welcome, post webinar, gated content, re-engagement), each by segment and lifecycle stage, and rebuild them as results come in.
  • Set up webinars end to end: registration form, Zuddl to Pardot sync, campaign IDs, reminders and follow up. Build form handlers, autoresponders and landing page forms on the Storyblok website.
  • Load lead lists from events, content syndication and paid social with the right campaign, source and consent, and run the request board: intake, SLA, QA before launch.
  • Run optimization: Test subject lines, send times and content on every sequence, and personalise by segment, industry and stage.
  1. Marketing systems and integrations
  • Administer Pardot and its Salesforce sync: users, connectors, field mapping, campaign member status.
  • Own the links between Pardot, Salesforce, the website, Zuddl, Knak, Meta and Google Ads, and keep them documented.
  • Run Clay: enrichment waterfalls, list building, and the signal tables that feed BDR routing.
  • Evaluate, buy and renew the marketing tools.
  1. Lead flow, scoring and routing operations
  • Own lead scoring and grading, reviewed each quarter against what converts.
  • Own lifecycle stages (MQL, SAL, SQL) and the handoff rules to BDRs and AEs.
  • Bring self serve signups and product usage into scoring and lifecycle, so PLG and enterprise leads run through 1 flow.
  1. Data quality and reporting
  • Own the sync between the warehouse, Pardot and Salesforce in both directions: enrichment and usage in, campaign results out.
  • Keep the database clean: dedupe, normalise country, industry and title, suppression lists, GDPR consent by region.
  • Own the campaign taxonomy and UTM standard across the team and agencies.
  • Write your own SQL and Salesforce reports; build the funnel and pipeline by source reports in the BI tool. Watch deliverability: domain health, bounce and spam rates.
  1. Automation, process and cadence
  • Template the repeat request types (list uploads, list emails, form handlers, webinar setups), then automate them with Clay, LLM tools (Claude) and a workflow builder such as n8n or Zapier, each with QA and a fallback.
  • Write the playbooks and train marketers to self serve where it is safe.
  • Own and report monthly on speed to lead, MQL to SQL and form to meeting rates, request turnaround and share automated, database coverage and accuracy, and email deliverability and engagement.

EDUCATION AND EXPERIENCE

  • Bachelor's degree in Marketing, Business, Information Systems or a related field, or equivalent practical experience.
  • 4+ years in marketing operations or marketing automation, ideally in B2B SaaS.
  • Pardot administration: Salesforce connector, Engagement Studio, form handlers, campaign sync. Salesforce fluency: campaigns, lead and contact fields, reports and dashboards.
  • Built nurture programs and can show what they did to conversion.
  • Ran a request queue at volume and cut the time per request with templates and automation; shipped automations with a workflow builder and LLM tools and kept them running.
  • Lead scoring, lifecycle and routing experience, with results.
  • Working SQL, and hands on with an enrichment tool such as Clay, ZoomInfo or Apollo.
  • Knowledge of GDPR and consent management across regions.
  • Demonstrates AI literacy, with the ability to leverage AI tools to enhance productivity, decision-making, and quality of work while applying sound judgment and critical thinking.

PROFESSIONAL COMPETENCIES

  • Operating discipline: runs a high volume request queue on an SLA with QA before every launch.
  • Systems thinking: sees how Pardot, Salesforce, the website, Clay and the warehouse connect, and keeps the data flowing cleanly between them.
  • Data fluency: pulls own numbers, defines fields and builds reports without an analyst in between.
  • Automation mindset: templates repeat work, then builds the automation that removes it.
  • Attention to detail: a wrong list or a broken form reaches thousands of people.
  • Testing habit: runs tests on every sequence and changes the program based on results.
  • Cross-functional coordination: aligns Demand Gen, Events, Content, Partner Marketing, Sales Development and Sales Ops around 1 lead flow.
  • Communication: writes clear playbooks and guidance that marketers follow without a meeting.

#7587. TRM Labs: Senior / Staff Threat Intelligence Analyst, Hacks

Headquarters: United States
URL: https://www.trmlabs.com/

About the Role

When a protocol is drained, or an exchange is compromised, our customers need to know within minutes or hours which addresses to block and where the funds are going. You will own TRM's answer to that, end-to-end.

As a Senior / Staff Threat Intelligence Analyst, you will lead TRM's hacks category: the metrics, the system that hits them, and the small contractor team that keeps it running around the clock. We align on the outcome; the approach is yours.

The impact you will have

  • Take command when a big hack breaks and act as a leader. Run the response: triage, coordination across teams, internal and customer communications, and the incident report afterwards.

  • Get every major hack into TRM fast. Make sure every major hack is visible to customers within hours of the first credible public report, around the clock.

  • Keep TRM's hack data complete and accurate. Every hack, at any size, is recorded with its entity, theft addresses, date, amount, and hack type.

  • Follow the money at scale. Design the system, with our Data Science and engineering teams, that carries hack-specific attribution downstream as stolen funds move.

  • Build the machine with AI. Leverage AI to build the tools and agents to automate key parts of hacks analysis and response, while ensuring human quality control over every output.

  • Explain how it happened. Root-cause the most important hacks and publish best-in-industry technical analysis that makes TRM the first source cited.

  • See the next one coming. Build approaches that spot vulnerable contracts before they are exploited, and get them into our product.

What we're looking for

  • 5+ years of professional experience in blockchain intelligence, crypto investigations, cyber threat intelligence, incident response, or a closely related field.

  • Has owned incident response in a high-stakes environment (exchange, security firm, incident response, or CTI team): war rooms, incident reports, communications under pressure.

  • Strong blockchain tracing across major chains (BTC, EVM, TRON, Solana), including bridges, cross-chain swaps, and mixers.

  • Understands how DeFi, bridge, and exchange exploits work, and how attackers behave after the theft.

  • Applied AI fluency. We expect you to already be building AI-assisted or agentic workflows in your daily analytical work, to be able to show how you validate their outputs and where they fail, and to treat AI as a force multiplier.

  • Experience managing contractors or leading a small team.

  • Writes and briefs clearly, from technical deep-dives to executive summaries.

  • Hands-on and highly accountable: does the hardest work, not only coordinates it.

Nice to have

  • Smart-contract reverse-engineering (Solidity).

  • Experience at a security auditor or blockchain analytics firm.

  • Experience following specific well-known hacker groups.

About the Team

The hacks function sits within TRM's Primary Intelligence organization, alongside the Facilitation & Money Laundering (F&ML) pod. You will work closely with the Nation States, Cyber Threat Intelligence, and Global Investigations teams, as well as with Data Science and Engineering on automation, and with Marketing on publications.

Team Operating Rhythms

  • Monthly metrics review with your manager.

  • Weekly operating sync with your team.

  • Regular syncs with your team across time zones.

  • A war room, run by you, whenever a major hack breaks.

Application Instructions

If you’re interested in joining TRM, we encourage you to apply directly. Every application is reviewed by our Talent team.

Before applying, review the job description carefully and highlight the experience and impact that best demonstrate the required qualifications. Please also provide thoughtful and accurate answers to the application questions, as these will be used to evaluate your skills and qualifications for the role.

If you send your resume directly to someone at TRM, we can’t guarantee it will reach the appropriate hiring team. Applying directly is the best way to ensure your application will be considered.

Interviewing with TRM

Our process is designed to understand how you think, solve problems, and deliver impact, while allowing you to evaluate TRM. Most interview processes include a case study, an AI skills assessment, and a Leadership Principles interview.

  • Recruiter Intro: Qualifications, motivations, and alignment with the role.

  • Hiring Manager: Deep dive into your relevant experience, skills, and impact.

  • First Round: 1–2 interviews focused on the skills most critical to the role.

  • Final Round: Hands-on case study. This is usually a panel-style session where we go deeper into your craft, problem-solving, and alignment with TRM.

  • References: Former colleagues who can provide insight into your work and impact.

  • Offer: Congrats! Your recruiter will present the offer and compensation details.

  • Welcome to TRM: We're excited to build a safer world with you!

Your recruiter will share your specific interview plan and preparation guidance along the way.

AI Fluency at TRM

AI fluency is a baseline expectation at TRM.

We believe AI meaningfully changes how top performers operate. We expect every team member to use AI to accelerate and reimagine their craft, not just automate surface tasks.

At TRM, AI fluency means you are among the top 10 percent of operators in your function in how you apply AI to:

  • Accelerate repeatable workflows

  • Structure and solve problems

  • Improve output quality

  • Increase speed and leverage

You will be evaluated on applied AI fluency during the interview process.

Life at TRM

We are building a safer world. That promise shows up in how we work every day.

TRM moves FAST. We are a high-velocity, high-ownership team that expects clarity, follow-through, and impact. People who thrive here are energized by hard problems, experimentation, and continuous feedback. If something takes months elsewhere, it will ship here in days.

Our work sits at the intersection of AI, national security, and fighting crime. The problems are complex, the stakes are real, and the environment evolves quickly. The pace and intensity of the work reflect the importance of the mission. As a result, the way we operate requires a high level of ownership, adaptability, collaboration, and creative problem-solving.

At TRM, you should expect:

  • Priorities and targets to change quickly

  • Operating with a high degree of ambiguity

  • A high level of personal ownership and accountability

  • Close collaboration across teams and functions

  • Frequent, high-touch communication

  • Creative problem-solving and out-of-the-box thinking

  • A pace that rewards urgency, adaptability, and outcomes

This environment is energizing for people who enjoy building, solving hard problems, and making progress in situations that are not always fully defined. It also requires thriving in ambiguity, adjusting course as new information emerges, and maintaining focus and positivity in a fast-moving and intense environment.

We also recognize that this style of operating is not for everyone. If you are primarily optimizing for predictability or a consistently balanced workload, we encourage you to use the interview process to pressure-test whether this environment is truly the right fit. We want teammates who thrive here, not just survive here.

If you are excited by meaningful problems, motivated by ambitious goals, and energized by working alongside mission-driven colleagues, there is a good chance you will find TRM to be an exceptional place to grow and contribute.

Leadership Principles

We hire and grow against three leadership principles. They’re the standards for how we operate, treat each other, and make decisions.

  • Impact-Oriented Trailblazer: We put customers first and move with speed, focus, and adaptability. We treat every plan like an experiment – test, ship, measure, and iterate quickly.

  • Master Craftsperson: We care deeply about our craft. We balance speed with high standards, own outcomes end‑to‑end, and invest in getting better everyday.

  • Inspiring Colleague: We add clarity and energy, not noise. We bring humility, candor, and a one‑team mindset — giving and receiving feedback to make the team stronger.

Join our Mission

At TRM, we care deeply about our craft. We are looking for individuals who want their work to matter, who experiment with speed and rigor, and who take pride in building a safer world for billions of people. If you’re excited by TRM’s mission but don’t check every box, we encourage you to apply — we hire for slope, judgment, and the will to learn fast.

TRM is a Series C company with $220M in total funding, backed by Goldman Sachs, Bessemer, Y Combinator, Thoma Bravo, and others. Headquartered in San Francisco, TRM operates as a distributed-first company with hubs in Los Angeles, San Francisco, New York, Washington D.C., London, and Singapore.

#7588. HubSpot : Lead Counsel, Public Company Governance & Corporate Programs

Headquarters: United States
URL: https://www.hubspot.com/

Lead Counsel, Public Company Governance & Corporate Programs

Legal

Remote - USA

POS-20140

The Legal Team

HubSpot's Legal team is a proactive, business-enabling partner — not a back-office function. We help our teams move fast, smart, and responsibly, balancing innovation with rigor so HubSpot can scale as a publicly traded, AI-native company while maintaining trust, compliance, and clarity.

Role Summary

This is a role for a senior governance attorney who has earned the confidence of boards and committees, not just supported them. You will be HubSpot's primary governance lawyer — owning the company's governance policy framework, the NG&S Committee, the operational backbone for all three Board committees, proxy governance and annual meeting, and governance-focused investor engagement. 

The governance attorney who succeeds here doesn't just maintain recurring programs — they actively improve how those programs work, bringing the same rigor to process design as to legal advice. 

HubSpot is building an AI-first legal function, and we want someone who sees that as an opportunity to do governance work at a higher level of quality and scale, not as an obligation to satisfy.

This is an individual contributor role reporting to the Director of Corporate Legal.

What You'll Do

1. Lead HubSpot's Governance Policy Framework

  • Own HubSpot's governance policy framework, including committee charters, governance practices, director independence analysis, related-party frameworks, and Board refresh and skills matrix legal analysis.
  • Lead the annual governance policy refresh, ensuring HubSpot's practices remain aligned with evolving NYSE requirements and governance best practices — and anticipating changes before they arrive.

2.  Own the Nominating, Governance & Sustainability Committee

  • Serve as the substantive legal owner for the NG&S Committee, preparing materials, advising on agenda items, translating legal and governance requirements into practical recommendations, and supporting the committee chair.
  • Advise on director independence, related-party considerations, Board refresh, and skills matrix analysis, with the judgment to resolve most questions independently.

3. Run Board and Committee Operations

  • Own the operational backbone for the Board and its Audit, Compensation, and NG&S Committees as a shared service across all three committees.
  • Own the governance sections of HubSpot's annual proxy statement, including director biographies, skills matrix, committee descriptions, governance policy disclosures, and ESG and sustainability content, and lead the annual meeting process and logistics end-to-end.

4. Lead Governance-Focused Investor Engagement

  • Lead governance-focused investor engagement, including proxy advisor outreach related to Board composition and governance practices, and partner with Investor Relations on shareholder communications and proxy season strategy.
  • Engage directly with ISS, Glass Lewis, and significant shareholders on governance matters with the preparation and credibility to represent HubSpot's positions authoritatively.

5. Own Corporate Governance Programs

  • Own recurring corporate governance programs (governance filings, D&O questionnaires, governance surveys, and related compliance obligations) with disciplined execution and strong records practice.

6. Build an AI-First Governance Function

  • You have a point of view on how AI and automation can raise the quality and efficiency of recurring governance work, and you act on it. You will identify and implement process improvements (including AI-enabled workflows) that reduce friction, strengthen governance operations, and increase the team's operating leverage.
  • Build templates, playbooks, and governance infrastructure that make this function more scalable and resilient over time.

What You'll Bring

Required Qualifications

  • 8-10+ years of corporate and securities law experience at top law firms and/or in-house, with at least 3-4 years of meaningful in-house experience advising a U.S. public company on corporate governance matters.
  • Deep knowledge of public company governance (Board and committee practices, director independence, governance policies, stock exchange requirements, and related-party frameworks) with the judgment to own governance calls independently.
  • Extensive experience supporting Boards and Board committees, with the credibility to advise on governance matters directly and translate legal requirements into practical, boardroom-ready recommendations.
  • Meaningful ownership of, or a senior role in, annual proxy statement preparation and annual shareholder meeting processes.
  • Demonstrated track record of governance-related investor and proxy advisor engagement; able to partner with Investor Relations and senior stakeholders as a peer, not a process manager.
  • Track record of independently managing complex, recurring governance programs with strong attention to legal judgment, execution, and records discipline — and a drive to continuously improve how those programs run.
  • JD from an accredited law school and active bar membership in at least one U.S. jurisdiction.

Nice-to-Have Qualifications

  • Experience working in-house at a publicly traded technology or SaaS company.
  • Experience engaging directly with proxy advisors, including ISS and Glass Lewis, on governance matters.
  • Experience advising on ESG or sustainability-related governance and public disclosures.
  • Hands-on experience using or building AI-enabled workflows in legal or governance contexts, with a clear point of view on where AI adds real leverage in governance work.

Where You'll Work

  • Location: United States
  • Work location preference eligibility: Remote
  • Travel/Shift: No travel or shift requirements

Application Requirements

  • There is no application deadline for this role.
  • Applications and resumes/CVs must be submitted in English.
  • Cover letters are not required.

Pay & Benefits

The cash compensation below includes base salary, on-target commission for employees in eligible roles, and annual bonus targets under HubSpot’s bonus plan for eligible roles. In addition to cash compensation, some roles are eligible to participate in HubSpot’s equity plan to receive restricted stock units (RSUs). Some roles may also be eligible for overtime pay. Individual compensation packages are tailored to your skills, experience, qualifications, and other job-related reasons.

At HubSpot, fair compensation practices aren’t just about checking off the box for legal compliance. It’s about living out our value of transparency with our employees, candidates, and community.

$165,600—$265,000 USD

Your Team and Role

Working on the Backend Functional Team, you'll lead complex projects across key company-wide objectives. Using Perl (Version 5), Go, and Node.js, you’ll write pragmatic, performant, testable code that works consistently, and work on related projects. Recent projects include:

As a Senior Backend Engineer, you'll collaborate remotely with your peers and take responsibility for solving product issues impacting our all-in-one privacy solution, whether large or small. In time, you'll formally mentor other engineers, supporting their career growth and the growth of the Backend Team at DuckDuckGo.

 

About You

  • 7+ years of professional experience, primarily focused on backend systems that power consumer-facing products

#7769. Solutions Architect at LiveKit

ABOUT LIVEKIT

LiveKit is building the infrastructure layer for the agentic era of computing. Our platform gives developers everything they need to build, test, deploy, scale, and observe AI agents in production. Founded in 2021, LiveKit powers voice and agentic AI applications for OpenAI, Salesforce, Spotify, Meta, and tens of thousands of other developers, collectively facilitating billions of calls each year.

ABOUT THIS ROLE

We're hiring a Solutions Architect (Pre-Sales) to be the technical partner to the engineers and enterprises building on LiveKit.

You'll work alongside an account executive to understand what a customer is trying to build, design the right architecture, and bring it to life with live demos and proofs-of-concept — guiding teams from first conversation to production.

This role is ideal for someone who loves to build, is energized by talking with other engineers, and takes pride in turning a hard technical problem into something that just works. Real-time voice and video is visceral — latency, quality, and reliability are felt, not read about — so the best conversations include something live. You'll collaborate closely with our Sales, Engineering, Product teams to win technical decisions and to make sure what you hear in the field informs how we build.

YOU'LL THRIVE HERE IF YOU:

  • love to build and solve the most challenging customer problems with LiveKit

  • have a curious mind with a continuous desire to learn and improve

  • enjoy demoing and presenting, whether to a room of engineer, to a CTO or at a conference

  • have a team first mentally, seeking to lift others around you as you progress in your career

WHAT YOU'LL DO

  • Partner with an account executive as the technical lead on strategic deals — from discovery and architecture design through demos, POCs, and technical decisions

  • Advise customers on cloud architecture and deployment across self-hosted and LiveKit Cloud (AWS, GCP, Azure)

  • Build live demos and reference applications — voice agents, video pipelines, telephony integrations — tailored to each customer's use case, and reusable by the wider team

  • Scope and run proofs-of-concept against clear, shared success criteria, guiding customers all the way to production

  • Reason with customers about connectivity, infrastructure, and end-to-end latency to design for scale and reliability

  • Create technical content — blog posts, guides, webinars, and conference talks — that helps developers and enterprises build with LiveKit

  • Act as the technical enabler for your region's pod, and share field insight with Product and Engineering to help shape the roadmap

WHO YOU ARE

  • 4+ years in solutions architecture, sales engineering, or a customer-facing engineering role (or equivalent hands-on engineering experience with strong customer instincts)

  • Hands-on experience with at least one major cloud provider (AWS, GCP, or Azure) — you can architect, deploy, and debug real applications

  • Comfortable with the modern deployment toolchain: containers, Kubernetes, infrastructure-as-code, and networking fundamentals (load balancing, DNS, firewalls/NAT)

  • Able to reason about connectivity, infrastructure, and latency end to end — how architecture, network path, compute, co-location, and model choice shape the AI agentic experiences of today

  • Strong programming ability in at least one of our core SDK languages — Python, TypeScript/Node, or Go

  • A clear communicator who can demo, present, and write for technical audiences

NICE TO HAVE

  • Working knowledge of real-time media protocols and their latency/quality trade-offs (WebRTC, RTP/SRTP, WebSockets)

  • Familiarity with the AI voice stack: STT/TTS providers, LLM integration, speech-to-speech models, turn detection, and telephony/SIP

  • Experience speaking at meetups or conferences, or a track record of technical writing or open-source contributions

OUR COMMITMENT TO YOU

  • An opportunity to build something truly impactful to the world

  • Contribute to open source alongside world-class engineers

  • Competitive salary and equity package

  • Flexible vacation policy

#7770. Software Engineer - Storage at Supabase

About Supabase

Supabase is the Postgres development platform, built by developers for developers. We provide a complete backend solution including Database, Auth, Storage, Edge Functions, Realtime, and Vector Search. All services are deeply integrated and designed for growth.

About the Role

Supabase Storage is a scalable and high performance object storage service and is one of our fastest growing services. The current landscape of tools for uploading, storing, processing and delivering assets is complicated. Developers are forced to think about regions, caching, codecs and security before they can start uploading anything.

We are looking for engineers to solve this problem once and for all!

You will be working across the entire stack - client libraries, CLI, CDN, the storage server and the deployment infrastructure. One day, you might focus on improving upload resilience in iOS applications; the next, you could be optimizing the S3 protocol for efficient retries or finding ways to accelerate upload processing.

You will be contributing to both our open source server and upstream dependencies we use, like the TUS server.

This role is ideal for someone who thrives in async, fast-paced environments and is excited about building developer tools that scale to millions.

What You’ll Be Responsible For

  • Improving our analytics bucket offering and improving the storage of open file formats like iceberg and ducklake

  • Asset optimization to speed up delivery for websites and mobile applications

  • Building out our edge network to speed up uploads and build features like automatic cache invalidation

  • Improve the performance, observability, and reliability of our storage server

  • Work with the frontend team to build a fast, powerful storage explorer

  • Improve our APIs, client libraries and CLI to provide an exceptional end-to-end DX for our users

  • Resolve escalated support issues, while also identifying and resolving systemic issues across the platform

You Might Be a Good Fit If You

  • Have 5+ years backend engineering experience of running Node.js in production

  • Have experience with highly scalable, distributed systems

  • Are comfortable with working across the stack - backend application development, some infrastructure work including deploying, monitoring and scaling the service

  • Posses familiarity with observability tools (OpenTelemetry, Prometheus, Grafana) and performance tuning

  • Have experience with production-grade PostgreSQL usage, including tuning and troubleshooting

  • Demonstrate an ownership mentality: driving projects end-to-end

  • Display empathy towards developer experience

Nice-to-have

  • Prior experience working on developer tools

  • Solid knowledge of cloud infrastructure (AWS, GCP or Azure) and containerization (Docker, Kubernetes)

  • Direct experience designing and operating large file upload/download pipelines at scale

  • Deep, practical CDN integration and optimization knowledge

  • Experience with image and video processing tools (sharp, dssim, ffmpeg), formats and codecs (AVIF, WebM, Brotli) is a plus

  • Experience with the frontend ecosystem (Next.js, React, etc) is a plus

What We Offer

  • Fully Remote

    We hire globally. We believe you can do your best work from anywhere. There are no Supabase offices, but we provide a WeWork membership or co-working allowance you can use anywhere in the world.

  • ESOP

    Every team member receives ESOP (equity ownership) in the company. We want everyone to share in the upside of what we’re building together.

  • Tech Allowance

    Use this budget to set up your ideal work environment—laptop, monitor, headphones, or whatever helps you do your best work.

  • Health Benefits

    Supabase covers 100% of health insurance for employees and 80% for dependents, wherever you are. Your wellbeing and your family’s health are important to us.

  • Annual Off-Sites

    Once a year, the entire company gathers in a new city for a week of connection, collaboration, and fun. It’s a highlight of our year.

  • Flexible Work

    We operate asynchronously and trust you to manage your own time. You know what needs to be done and when.

  • Professional Development

    Every team member receives an annual education allowance to spend on learning—courses, books, conferences, or anything that supports your growth.

About the Team

Supabase was born-remote and open-source-first. We believe our globally distributed team is our secret weapon in building tools developers love.

  • ~400 team members

  • 60+ countries

  • 20+ languages spoken

  • Over $1B raised (including our $500M Series F)

  • 540,000+ community members

We move fast, build in public, and use what we ship. If it’s in your project, we probably use it in ours too. We believe deeply in the open-source ecosystem and strive to support—not replace—existing tools and communities.

#7772. Senior Payroll Specialist at LiveKit

About LiveKit

LiveKit is building the infrastructure layer for the voice-driven era of computing. Our platform gives developers everything they need to build, test, deploy, scale, and observe agents in production. Founded in 2021, LiveKit powers voice AI applications for OpenAI, xAI, Salesforce, Coursera, Spotify, and thousands of others, collectively facilitating billions of calls each year.

About You

LiveKit's team is growing fast, in the US and around the world, and Finance is taking over payroll. You'll be the person who makes it run: every employee, every state, every country, every filing.
Most senior payroll roles hand you a well-oiled machine and ask you not to break it. This one asks you to build it. You'll own US and international payroll, set up the calendar and controls, assist with month end close, and be the internal expert on anything that touches a paycheck.

You'll thrive as the Senior Payroll Specialist if you:

  • are obsessive about tying out numbers to the penny

  • are known as the go-to person for tackling tough problems

  • work hard and can build and ship fast

  • are focused on polish, detail and quality

  • are a fast learner, frequently picking up new tips, tricks, and skills

The best way to impress us is with thoughtful ways you'd implement LiveKit, and potentially tinkering with it 😊

What You'll Do

As a Senior Payroll Specialist at LiveKit, you will:

  • Run US and international payroll end to end, from collecting inputs to final reconciliation

  • Own all associated payroll tax filings, domestic and international, and keep us compliant and on time

  • Manage our PEO and EOR relationships, and know where their responsibility ends and yours begins

  • Reconcile payroll to the general ledger each cycle and support month-end close

  • Build and maintain our payroll calendar, processes, and documentation as payroll moves into Finance

  • Partner with People/Talent and accounting on new hires, changes, terminations, and month-end reconciliation

  • Support equity administration in Carta and equity reporting for the board

  • Be the go-to contact for payroll questions internally and for auditor requests externally

Who You Are

  • Hands-on experience running both US and international payroll, including Canada and/or the EU

  • Experience managing payroll tax filings directly, not just handing them off to a provider

  • Experience with multi-state US payroll for a remote, distributed team

  • Strong experience working within PEO and/or EOR structures

  • Familiarity with startup-focused platforms such as Rippling, Deel, Remote, or similar

  • Comfort working independently as the subject-matter expert, juggling many moving pieces and flagging tax, legal, and compliance risk before it becomes a problem

Nice to Have

  • CPP, FPC, or a similar payroll certification

  • Experience moving payroll in-house, switching providers, or migrating an HRIS

  • Experience with equity administration in Carta

  • Experience at a fast-growing company with a distributed, international team

  • You've personally caught a payroll error before anyone else did, and you still think about it fondly

Our Commitment to You

  • An opportunity to build something truly impactful to the world

  • Contribute to open source alongside world-class engineers

  • Competitive salary and equity package

  • Health, dental, and vision benefits

  • Flexible vacation policy

#7774. Senior Product Designer at DuckDuckGo

Who We Are

Hi, we're DuckDuckGo, the online protection company and remote-first team of 400+ on a mission to raise the standard of trust online. Founded in 2008 and profitable since 2014, our annual revenue is in the hundreds of millions USD and millions use our browser on Mac, Windows, iOS, and Android, our search engine, and the DuckDuckGo subscription. We also offer private, useful, and optional AI, including Duck.ai, which lets you chat privately with ChatGPT, Claude, and other AIs, all in one place.

 

Our culture of trust, inclusivity, and empowered project management underpins everything we do, where each team member takes full ownership of their projects, from scoping and execution to postmortem. If you're seeking end-to-end ownership of your work, you've come to the right place!

Your Team and Role

In this role, you will collaborate with team members at all levels to identify and solve problems across our current product offering, as well as helping to pioneer new solutions as we build out the privacy layer for the Internet. You'll have the opportunity to level up our products through exploring and testing concepts that improve overall experience and increase delight while appealing to a broader audience.

Applying your hands-on design skills, you will autonomously lead highly impactful projects that scale across millions of users. From ideation to execution, you'll assess vague problems, propose high-impact solutions, and execute them against a set of success criteria. You'll have a voice in all product-related design decisions, whether large or small, and an opportunity to work on a team dedicated to our ethical, privacy by design approach. Read more about Design at DuckDuckGo on our blog: https://spreadprivacy.com/ethical-by-design/

About You

  • 6+ years of experience in product design roles, including 2+ years in senior-level design positions at significant global consumer brands.

  • Proficiency in managing projects of varying complexity, from proposal, through execution and release, all while working transparently and collaborating with cross-functional stakeholders and contributors.

  • A knack for pushing creative limits while solving challenging design problems and delivering on delight, craft and exceptional quality.

  • Ability to communicate visual and interaction recommendations with high-quality mockups, prototypes, and rationale.

  • Accustomed to applying agentic solutions into every day project management and design execution workflows to both accelerate and raise the quality of your work.

  • Ability to write creative briefs that clearly define and communicate project goals, rationale, and success metrics with stakeholders.

  • Proficiency in research and experimentation — you can leverage both qualitative and quantitative data in driving decisions forward.

  • Skills to manage ambiguity and wrangle vague problems, propose innovative solutions, and execute them with a focus on metrics.

  • Ability to take initiative in generating ideas and carrying them forward with urgency and discretion, while working openly and collaboratively.

  • A willingness to apply your experience and expertise in educating, guiding, and supporting others, while serving as a steward to maintain quality of execution across designated areas of design.

  • Experience participating in multiple areas of the product process including prioritization, strategy, and user testing.

  • United States: $190,500 USD

  • United Kingdom: £134,600 GBP

  • Canada: CA$253,500 CAD

  • All other regions*: €157,350 EUR

Eligibility for company-sponsored health benefits is limited to team members based in the United States. This program does not extend to team members located in other countries, such as Canada or the UK.

Disclosure Statement: Use of AI in Hiring Process

As part of our commitment to enhancing our recruitment process, we utilize artificial intelligence (AI) technology to assist in reviewing and summarizing job applications and test projects, including those tools integrated into our recruitment vendor platforms. We use AI to flag potentially fraudulent applications, analyze and summarize applicants’ experience, interviews, and project performance, and help streamline our selection process.

Key Principles:

  1. Data Privacy: All information provided in your application will be handled in accordance with our Recruiting Privacy Policy. We ensure that your personal information is protected and used solely for recruitment purposes.

  2. Human Oversight and Accountability: The AI technology is designed to support our hiring team by providing insights and summaries of applications and evaluations of test projects against scoring rubrics. All final evaluations and hiring decisions, however, will be made by our hiring team, who will consider the AI's input alongside other factors.

  3. Transparency: We believe in transparency regarding our hiring practices. If you have any questions about how AI is used in our recruitment process, please feel free to reach out to us.

By submitting your application, you acknowledge and consent to the use of AI technology in our review process. If you would like to request an alternative selection process, please contact us as at careers@duckduckgo.com. Thank you for your interest in joining DuckDuckGo!

Every person on our team supports each other and does what we can to ensure we all are successful. 

Check out our website for more information: cloudlinux.com

Imunify360 Security Suite is an innovative security solution designed specifically for shared and VPS/Dedicated servers. The automated, easy-to-use solution with the six-layer approach to security delivers comprehensive and complete attack prevention.

We are looking for a Lead Security Engineer - Vulnerability Response to join our Web Protection Team.

The challenge ahead

When a critical vulnerability in a popular web application or WordPress plugin goes public, attackers start exploiting it within hours. Most site owners can't patch that fast. We want our customers protected before the first attack reaches them, and we want them to know it.

That's the challenge we're hiring for:

  • AI-driven vulnerability response. Build and run a pipeline that detects new high and critical vulnerabilities, generates WAF rules with AI assistance, validates them, and ships them to production in a short window.
  • Protection plus public announcement, both automated. The same pipeline that ships the rule also produces the public advisory. Every critical vulnerability we cover is announced soon after the rule ships, so customers and the market see that Imunify closed it first.
  • Build on what works. An AI-assisted pipeline already runs for our WordPress protection plugin. The first big project is to extend this approach to ModSecurity/Coraza WAF rules.
  • Speed without breaking sites. On shared hosting, a rule that blocks legitimate traffic is a customer incident. Fast and precise both matter.
  • Make it measurable. Time-to-protection, coverage of critical vulnerabilities, and false-positive rate are the numbers this role owns.

About the role

You will own the vulnerability response pipeline end to end and be its main builder. You combine deep web application security expertise with strong automation skills, and you think about the product: what customers need, what to measure, what to build next. You'll lead a small team of security analysts and work closely with automation, QA, and marketing.

What you'll do

  • Design and build the AI-driven pipeline from disclosure to announcement: vulnerability intake, triage, rule drafting, testing, release, and the public advisory.
  • Lead the extension of our existing WordPress-plugin pipeline to ModSecurity/Coraza rules.
  • Write and review WAF rules (ModSecurity/SecLang) for real-world web vulnerabilities.
  • Build test harnesses that check rules against exploits and real traffic before release.
  • Lead and mentor security analysts: set priorities, review their work, remove blockers.
  • Define and track time-to-protection, coverage, and false-positive rate, and improve them.
  • Automate advisory generation as part of the pipeline, and agree the format and review flow with marketing.

Requirements

What we're looking for:

  • 5+ years in web/application security: vulnerability research, detection engineering, WAF engineering, or similar.
  • Deep understanding of web attack classes (SQLi, XSS, RCE, auth bypass, path traversal, file upload, deserialization) and how to detect them in HTTP traffic.
  • Hands-on experience writing WAF rules (ModSecurity/SecLang, OWASP CRS, or similar) and regular expressions.
  • Able to read PHP code and understand a vulnerability from an advisory or a patch diff.
  • Strong Python: you've built automation pipelines that run in production. CI/CD and automated testing.
  • Hands-on daily use of AI/LLM tools; you know where to trust them and how to verify their output.
  • Proven track record of leading a technical project end to end.
  • Product mindset: you reason about customer impact, not only technical correctness.
  • Upper-intermediate English or better.

Nice to have:

  • WordPress ecosystem security: plugin vulnerabilities, vulnerability feeds (Wordfence, Patchstack, WPScan), CVE credits or bug bounty.
  • Coraza or other WAF engines.
  • Exploit analysis or threat intelligence background.
  • Linux server administration, hosting industry.

Personal skills:

  • Strong ownership: you take a goal, not a task list.
  • See the whole process and find the bottleneck, not only fix individual issues.
  • Communicate clearly and raise risks early.
  • Calm under pressure: critical vulnerabilities don't wait for working hours.
  • Comfortable making decisions with incomplete information.

Benefits

What's in it for you?

  • A focus on professional development.
  • Interesting and challenging projects.
  • Fully remote work with flexible working hours, that allows you to schedule your day and work from any location worldwide.
  • Paid 24 days of vacation per year, 10 days of national holidays, and unlimited sick leaves.
  • Compensation for private medical insurance.
  • Co-working and gym/sports reimbursement.
  • Budget for education.
  • The opportunity to receive a reward for the most innovative idea that the company can patent.

#7776. Senior ML Engineer - Email Security (remote work) at CloudLinux

CloudLinux is a global remote-first company. We are driven by our principles: do the right thing, employees first, we are remote first, and we deliver high-volume, low-cost Linux infrastructure and security products that help companies to increase the efficiency of their operations. Every person on our team supports each other and does what we can to ensure we all are successful. 

Check out our website for more information: https://cloudlinux.com/

CloudLinux Inc. develops the Imunify family of security products, designed to protect hosting environments from a wide range of cyber threats. One of these solutions is Imunify Email, an email security product that protects hosting servers against spam, phishing, and other email-based threats.

Imunify Email filters mail on about 5,000 hosting servers. Today the filter is rspamd with rules and a scoring model we train in-house. We are replacing it with a transformer we trained ourselves that runs on the customer's own CPU in under 100 MB and already beats rspamd at every operating point we measured. We are looking for a Senior Machine Learning Engineer to join our team and work alongside the engineer currently responsible for both the existing spam-filtering pipeline and the new ML model. Together, you will drive the development, improvement, and production rollout of our email security technology.

What you will do:

  • Train, evaluate, and release spam-detection models: build the corpus, pick the threshold, read the precision/recall report, decide ship or retrain.
  • Build the production side of the new engine in Go: the inference service, the mail-filter integration, the tokenizer, the streaming message parser.
  • Fix detection quality end to end, from a customer's "this phishing got through" to a rule, a corpus fix, or a retrain, with the false-positive cost weighed against the catch.
  • Keep the corpus tooling and training infrastructure running.
  • Review and approve classifier, rule, and model changes, so no release waits on one person.
  • Answer partner engineers who ask why a message scored the way it did.
  • Write specs, handbooks, and post-mortems. That is how this team transfers knowledge.

Your first six months:

  • Month 1: you ship a rule-model release on your own.
  • Month 3: your first piece of the new engine is merged and running on staging.
  • Month 6: a model release goes out while the principal engineer is away.

Requirements

What you bring:

  • 5+ years of shipping detection or classification systems where false positives have a cost you had to defend: anti-spam, anti-phishing, anti-malware, fraud, abuse.
  • You have taken at least one ML classifier to production yourself: corpus construction, threshold selection, error analysis.
  • Hands-on transformer training in PyTorch: tokenizers, masked-language pre-training, fine-tuning, ONNX export, CPU inference measurement.
  • Linux, shell, and Python on shared servers and a shared GPU.
  • Daily usage of AI coding agents and tools.
  • English at upper-Intermediate (B2) level or higher, with particularly strong written communication skills for technical documentation, specifications, and code reviews. Your specs and merge-request descriptions count as much as your code.

It is a strong plus if you have:

  • Email internals: SMTP, MIME, transfer encodings, SPF / DKIM / DMARC, how Exim, Postfix, and Dovecot handle mail to a filter.
  • Experience in creating embedded models
  • Go or Rustfor systems code: parsers, streaming, memory budgets, concurrency.
  • rspamd or SpamAssassin internals: rules, composites, Lua plugins, mass-check scoring.
  • Multi-million-sample corpora, labelling pipelines, annotator tooling.
  • Small models for constrained hardware: quantization, distillation, latency budgets.
  • LLM-based labelers or validators for scaling annotation.
  • Hosting panels: cPanel, Plesk, DirectAdmin.

Benefits

What's in it for you?

  • A focus on professional development.
  • Interesting and challenging projects.
  • Fully remote work with flexible working hours, that allows you to schedule your day and work from any location worldwide.
  • Paid 24 days of vacation per year, 10 days of national holidays, and unlimited sick leaves.
  • Compensation for private medical insurance.

  • Budget for education.

#7777. Senior ML Engineer - Malware Detection (remote work) at CloudLinux

CloudLinux is a global remote-first company. We are driven by our principles: do the right thing, employees first, we are remote first, and we deliver high-volume, low-cost Linux infrastructure and security products that help companies to increase the efficiency of their operations. Every person on our team supports each other and does what we can to ensure we all are successful. 

Check out our website for more information: https://cloudlinux.com/

Imunify360 is CloudLinux's security suite for Linux web servers: real-time and background malware scanning, automated cleanup, a WAF, runtime PHP protection (Proactive Defense), a database malware scanner, and IP reputation. It runs on hosting providers' shared, VPS, and dedicated servers, so new web-malware campaigns land on our fleet early.

We sit on one of the largest labeled corpora of live web-server malware in the industry: files, verdicts, cleanup outcomes, and analyst decisions collected from production hosting servers every day. It already drives our signatures, heuristics, and ML models, and there is far more to get out of it. We are looking for a Senior ML Engineer who will turn that data into detection that ships — classifiers, LLM-assisted triage and generated signatures — and keep exploring new ML/LLM methods against it, measured by the product's false-negative and false-positive rates, not by notebook accuracy.

Our dataset focuses on web malware — PHP, JavaScript, HTML phishing kits, infected WordPress and Joomla plugins and themes, database injections — with the context around each file.

What you'll work with:

  • Malware samples from production servers, with verdicts and analyst decisions
  • Years of signatures and detection rules
  • Customer-reported false negatives and false positives
  • Runtime behavior of code on live sites
  • Web-attack traffic
  • File metadata and how files spread across servers
  • Cleanup outcomes, including what came back

What you will do:

The goal is 10x more malware detected at the same or lower false-positive rate. Hand-written rules and faster pipelines will not get there; models trained on our data might.

  • Decide which files on a server deserve a closer look, within tight CPU and memory budgets
  • Tell malicious code from benign at fleet scale and a sensible cost per file
  • Generate detection signatures automatically from new campaigns
  • Catch malware that only looks malicious in context, such as phishing pages and script injections
  • Route each file to the right detection engine
  • Make cleanup precise so sites stay clean afterwards
  • Prove each model improves detection before it ships

How we work:

You get an experimentation track, GPUs, and a direct line to leadership; in return, you bring numbers, not opinions.

  • Remote-first, async across UTC−5 to UTC+8. Decisions are written down (ADR-Lite) and public by default.
  • Hardware follows evidence: Each training direction starts with dedicated GPUs on our own bare metal; leadership has committed to fund more when the case is made with data.
  • Agentic engineering: Claude and Codex are the default tools, wired to GitLab, Jenkins, Redash, Jira, and Slite through MCP servers. We look at how engineers work with agents — context, specificity, review quality — not only at output.
  • Close to ground truth: You work daily with malware analysts, detection and infrastructure engineers, and the rest of our ML team.
  • Ship gates: No model or build goes out if it raises false positives or false negatives on our past-error sets. Blameless post-mortems within 24 hours.

What you'll deliver:

You own the path from raw samples to a model in production, and the numbers that prove it helped.

  • First month: A first model trained on production data and measured against today's engines — suspicious-file triage or a metadata model. The evaluation harness in place: splits by time and campaign so families don't leak, and a regression set from past FPs and FNs.
  • By month 3: Several models in production: some in shadow mode building evidence, some live and producing real detections, all gated on the past-error regression set. A training setup that keeps its GPUs busy. A written proposal (ADR) for the detection cascade, with cost per file and hardware needs.
  • By month 12: Models are a main source of new detections, reported as share of new detections and FN-rate trend. Retraining on fresh analyst and FN labels on a fixed cadence. Generated signatures inside the analyst workflow. Two or three parallel training directions that other engineers can run with your tooling.

Requirements

What you bring:

  • 5+ years building ML systems that ran in production, including 2+ in an adversarial domain: security, abuse, fraud, or spam.
  • You have trained and fine-tuned transformer models yourself — data preparation, tokenizing code, LoRA/QLoRA or full fine-tunes, distillation, quantization — not only called hosted APIs.
  • Classic ML depth: gradient boosting, linear models on sparse features, calibration, class imbalance, and judgement on when these beat an LLM.
  • Evaluation discipline: leakage-free splits by time and family, precision at a fixed false-positive budget, regression sets, and an honest account of why production will differ from offline.
  • Inference cost engineering: you have measured throughput and latency and made a model fit a CPU or memory budget (ONNX, llama.cpp, vLLM, int8/int4).
  • Strong Python, PyTorch and the Hugging Face stack; SQL on large analytical tables (ClickHouse or similar).
  • Familiarity with PHP or JavaScript, or willingness to pick it up working with our malware analysts.
  • You use AI coding agents daily and can show how.
  • English at upper-Intermediate (B2) level or higher, with particularly strong written communication for async work.

It is a strong plus if you have:

  • Web malware, webshells, phishing kits or WordPress security experience.
  • Models over code: AST or token features, code LLMs, graph models on program structure.
  • Malware clustering and similarity analysis.
  • Multi-GPU training on your own bare metal; MLflow or Weights & Biases; dataset versioning.
  • Go or Rust, for on-server inference.
  • Published work or talks on ML for security.

Benefits

What's in it for you?

  • A focus on professional development.
  • Interesting and challenging projects.
  • Fully remote work with flexible working hours, that allows you to schedule your day and work from any location worldwide.
  • Paid 24 days of vacation per year, 10 days of national holidays, and unlimited sick leaves.
  • Compensation for private medical insurance.
  • Co-working and gym/sports reimbursement.
  • Budget for education.
  • The opportunity to receive a reward for the most innovative idea that the company can patent.

By applying for this position, you consent to the processing of your personal data as described in our Privacy Policy (https://cloudlinux.com/candidate-privacy-notice), which provides detailed information on how we maintain and handle your data.

  • can clearly explain complex technical concepts to others

  • are a fast learner and thrive in ambiguity

  • Build a remediation dashboard that engineering and leadership read and act on.
